# Generated by eu5autobuild.governor_generator.
# Recommendation only: this file never constructs, removes, or destroys a building.

eu5ab_register_governor_alerts = {
	cmf_set_alert_open_window = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert window = eu5ab_governor_window }
	cmf_set_alert_open_window = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert window = eu5ab_governor_window }
	cmf_set_alert_open_window = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert window = eu5ab_governor_window }
	cmf_set_alert_open_window = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert window = eu5ab_governor_window }
}

eu5ab_prepare_governor_window = {
	eu5ab_governor_ensure_planner_schema = yes
	eu5ab_governor_check_transport_rules = yes
	eu5ab_naval_planner_check_transport_rules = yes
	# The CMF alert manager sets the matching VariableSystem key. This effect is
	# intentionally script-only so alert clicks also refresh saved diagnostics.
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_last_run_year } has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty } }
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
		eu5ab_schedule_governor_recalculation = yes
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_dirty_from_owner_change = {
	scope:winner ?= { if = { limit = { is_human = yes } set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty } }
	scope:loser ?= { if = { limit = { is_human = yes } set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty } }
}

eu5ab_governor_monthly_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_human = yes }
		eu5ab_governor_ensure_planner_schema = yes
		eu5ab_governor_check_transport_rules = yes
		eu5ab_naval_planner_check_transport_rules = yes
		# Enroll active scans restored from saves predating the watchdog.
		eu5ab_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
		eu5ab_naval_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh value = 0 } }
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh add = 1 }
		# Zero-valued counters can disappear. Copy the old shared cadence once,
		# then advance the naval counter independently on later monthly pulses.
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_counter_initialized } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh value = var:eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_counter_initialized }
		else_if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh } change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh add = 1 } }
		else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_local_migration_cooldown var:eu5ab_local_migration_cooldown > 0 } change_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_migration_cooldown subtract = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_migration_cooldown var:eu5ab_naval_migration_cooldown > 0 } change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_migration_cooldown subtract = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } var:eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh >= eu5ab_governor_refresh_months } set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } var:eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh >= eu5ab_governor_refresh_months } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty }
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled > 0
				NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
				OR = {
					has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
					has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
				}
			}
			eu5ab_schedule_governor_recalculation = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled <= 0 NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } }
			cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
			cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert }
			eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = yes
			remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
			eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_migration = yes
			remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_new_recommendation
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_new_recommendation
			remove_variable = eu5ab_local_notified_target
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_notified_target
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_schedule_governor_recalculation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled > 0
			NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_recalculation_scheduled }
		}
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_recalculation_scheduled
		trigger_event_silently = { id = eu5ab_governor.1 days = 1 }
	}
}

# Manual refresh runs while the game is paused. If a periodic refresh is already
# queued, its event is consumed on arrival so the expensive scan is not repeated.
eu5ab_governor_manual_recalculation = {
	eu5ab_governor_ensure_planner_schema = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_human = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } eu5ab_layout_abort = yes }
		# Refresh settings before any eligibility/scoring gate. A one-shot manual
		# job does not change the user's automatic-refresh preferences.
		eu5ab_sync_cmm_settings = yes
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_realert
		remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_window_requested


		eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_migration = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_recalculation_scheduled }
			set_variable = eu5ab_governor_skip_next_scheduled_event
		}
		eu5ab_governor_run_recalculation_now = yes
		# A click returns a complete result (or an explicit failure), never a
		# background layout job. Automatic refreshes still use bounded GUI pulses.
		eu5ab_layout_finish_manual = yes
		eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_recalculation = yes
		remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_realert
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_run_recalculation_now = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_human = yes
			OR = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run }
			NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
		}
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
		eu5ab_recalculate_governor_recommendations = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
		set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_clear_country_results = {
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_result_road_tier
	remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_new_recommendation
	# Naval results belong to their independent planner.
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_total_value value = 0 }
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_local_top_1_list
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_local_top_2_list
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_local_top_3_list
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_coverage
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
}

eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_results = {

	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_top_1_list
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_top_2_list
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_top_3_list
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_coverage
}

eu5ab_governor_clear_location_cache = {
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_local_speed
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_topography
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_topography_impact
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_vegetation
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_node_value
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_node_demand
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_population
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_potential_tax_base
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_development
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_edge_cost_factor
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_planned_road_cost
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_road_edge_base_cost
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_land_edge_base_cost
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_old_proximity
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_old_control
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_cached_old_max_control
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_cheap_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_predicted
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_depth
}

eu5ab_governor_prepare_location_value = {
	eu5ab_prepare_strait_connections = yes
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_local_speed value = modifier:local_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_topography value = eu5ab_governor_topography_penalty }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_topography_impact value = eu5ab_governor_topography_impact }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_vegetation value = eu5ab_governor_vegetation_penalty }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_population value = population }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_potential_tax_base value = eu5ab_governor_potential_tax_base }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_development value = development }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_node_value value = eu5ab_governor_cached_location_value }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_edge_cost_factor value = eu5ab_governor_edge_cost_factor }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_road_edge_base_cost value = eu5ab_governor_road_edge_base_cost }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_planned_road_cost value = eu5ab_governor_planned_road_cost }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_land_edge_base_cost value = eu5ab_governor_land_edge_base_cost }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_old_proximity value = proximity }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_old_control value = local_control }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_old_max_control value = max_control }
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_node_demand
		value = {
			value = 80
			subtract = proximity
			min = 0
			divide = 100
			multiply = var:eu5ab_gov_node_value
		}
	}
	root = {
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_total_value add = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_queue_new
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue } }
		set_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
		root = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail }
				var:eu5ab_gov_queue_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_next value = scope:eu5ab_gov_queue_new } }
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail value = scope:eu5ab_gov_queue_new }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_head value = scope:eu5ab_gov_queue_new }
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail value = scope:eu5ab_gov_queue_new }
			}
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_queue_relax_one = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head }
		var:eu5ab_gov_queue_head = { save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_current }
		scope:eu5ab_gov_current = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next }
				var:eu5ab_gov_queue_next = { save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_next }
				root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_head value = scope:eu5ab_gov_next } }
			}
			else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail } }
			remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
			remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_kind != 1 var:eu5ab_gov_depth < 6 } var:eu5ab_gov_predicted > 0 }
				save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_edge_from
				every_neighbor_location = {
					limit = {
						is_ownable = yes
						owner ?= root
						OR = {
							root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_kind = 2
							NOT = { eu5ab_is_strait_connection_to = { target = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from } }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation } }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = -1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = 0 }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = 99 }
						save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_newly_visited
						root = { eu5ab_governor_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_gov_newly_visited } }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_kind = 2 }
						set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_relaxed value = eu5ab_governor_land_edge_prediction }
					}
					else = { set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_relaxed value = eu5ab_governor_known_land_edge_prediction } }
					if = {
						limit = { local_var:eu5ab_gov_relaxed > var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_gov_relaxed }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = { value = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from.var:eu5ab_gov_depth add = 1 } }
						eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
					}
					remove_local_variable = eu5ab_gov_relaxed
				}

        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = { OR = { topography = lakes topography = high_lakes topography = salt_pans } }
            every_neighbor_location = {
                limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { this = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from } OR = { root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_kind != 1 scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from.var:eu5ab_gov_depth <= 4 } }
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation } var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation } }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = 99 }
                    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_newly_visited
                    root = { eu5ab_governor_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_gov_newly_visited } }
                }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed value = eu5ab_governor_lake_transit_prediction }
                if = {
                    limit = { local_var:eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed > var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = { value = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from.var:eu5ab_gov_depth add = 2 } }
                    eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
                }
                remove_local_variable = eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed
            }
        }

			}
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_evaluate_candidate = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
		clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation } }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
		}
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation add = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_value value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage value = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = { value = eu5ab_governor_current_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = 0 }
	root = { eu5ab_governor_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate } }
	eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
	root = {
		while = {
			limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head }
			count = { value = num_locations multiply = 4 min = 100 }
			eu5ab_governor_queue_relax_one = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head } set_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted }
		every_in_list = {
			variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
			if = {
				limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
				if = {
					limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_predicted > eu5ab_governor_comparison_proximity }
					if = {
						limit = { this ?= scope:eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate }
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain add = prev.eu5ab_governor_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					else = {
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain add = prev.eu5ab_governor_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_value add = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage add = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
			remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
		}
		clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
	}
	# Quantize each displayed component first so the one-decimal card values
	# add exactly to the displayed overall score.
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_gain
		value = { value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain add = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_coverage value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage }
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_value
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_prepare_local_cheap_score = {
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_cheap_score value = var:eu5ab_gov_node_demand }
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_local_cheap_candidate
	every_neighbor_location = {
		limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { eu5ab_is_strait_connection_to = { target = scope:eu5ab_local_cheap_candidate } } }
		scope:eu5ab_local_cheap_candidate = {
			change_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_cheap_score add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_demand multiply = 0.65 } }
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_evaluate_local_candidate = {
	root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_kind value = 1 } }
	eu5ab_governor_evaluate_candidate = yes
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_site_gain value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_surrounding_gain value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_coverage value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_coverage }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_score value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_gain }
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_coverage
}

# Store one Local Governor candidate in the next country-level Top 3 slot.
eu5ab_governor_store_local_rank = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate
	root = {
		if = {
			limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_rank_cursor = 0 }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_1 value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_local_top_1_list target = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_1_score value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_score }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_1_site_gain value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_site_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_1_surrounding_gain value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_surrounding_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_1_coverage value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_coverage }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_rank_cursor = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_2 value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_local_top_2_list target = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_2_score value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_score }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_2_site_gain value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_site_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_2_surrounding_gain value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_surrounding_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_2_coverage value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_coverage }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_3 value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_local_top_3_list target = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_3_score value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_score }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_3_site_gain value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_site_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_3_surrounding_gain value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_surrounding_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_top_3_coverage value = scope:eu5ab_local_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_local_coverage }
		}
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_rank_cursor add = 1 }
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_rank_local_results = {
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_rank_cursor value = 0 }
	ordered_owned_location = {
		limit = {
			eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes
			has_variable = eu5ab_local_score
			var:eu5ab_local_score > 0
			root = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed } }
		}
		order_by = var:eu5ab_local_score
		max = 3
		check_range_bounds = no
		eu5ab_governor_store_local_rank = yes
	}
	remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_rank_cursor
}

eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = {
	cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration
}

eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_migration = {
	cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
}


eu5ab_governor_evaluate_local_alerts = {
	remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_new_recommendation
	if = {
		limit = { eu5ab_fresh_local_governor_slot_available = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1
				var:eu5ab_local_top_1_score >= eu5ab_governor_new_recommendation_threshold
				# The vanilla can-build trigger requires scope:actor and is invalid in
				# this background country effect. Slot capacity is checked above; this
				# rechecks the actor-independent location conditions instead.
				var:eu5ab_local_top_1 = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes }
			}
			set_variable = eu5ab_local_has_new_recommendation
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_realert
						NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_local_notified_target }
						var:eu5ab_local_notified_target != var:eu5ab_local_top_1
					}
				}
				cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_notified_target value = var:eu5ab_local_top_1 }
				cmf_show_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
			}
		}
		else = {
			cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
			remove_variable = eu5ab_local_notified_target
		}
	}
	else = {
		cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
		remove_variable = eu5ab_local_notified_target
		# Local migration is published by the complete-layout planner.
	}
}


# Country-scope master pass. It is event driven when an exposed on-action is
# available and periodically repeated to catch road-level/global modifier
# changes without a native invalidation hook, including expiring effects.
eu5ab_governor_snapshot_local_slot_state = {
	# All four native types have one level. In-progress buildings occupy slots
	# but do not add a completed source. Keep raw counts for diagnostics.
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_capacity value = effective_num_local_governors }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_lieutenancies value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_itinerant_seats value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_kings_manors value = 0 }
	every_owned_location = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:local_governor any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:local_governor building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:lieutenancy any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:lieutenancy building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_lieutenancies add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:itinerant_court_seat any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:itinerant_court_seat building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_itinerant_seats add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:kings_manor any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:kings_manor building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_kings_manors add = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_snapshot_local_diagnostics = {
	# Preserve native inputs and synchronous outcomes before layout_begin clears
	# the location caches. These observations never drive recommendation rules.
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_capacity value = effective_num_local_governors }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_occupied value = total_building_levels_including_construction:local_governor }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_lieutenancies value = total_building_levels_including_construction:lieutenancy }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_other_slots value = eu5ab_governor_local_other_slots }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_threshold value = eu5ab_governor_new_recommendation_threshold }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_slot_available value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { eu5ab_local_governor_slot_available = yes } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_slot_available value = 1 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_fresh_occupied value = var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_fresh_lieutenancies value = var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_lieutenancies }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_fresh_other_slots value = eu5ab_governor_fresh_local_other_slots }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_fresh_slot_available value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { eu5ab_fresh_local_governor_slot_available = yes } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_fresh_slot_available value = 1 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_slot_cache_mismatch value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_sync_slot_available != var:eu5ab_gov_sync_fresh_slot_available } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_slot_cache_mismatch value = 1 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_baseline_failed value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_baseline_failed value = 1 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_queue_exhausted value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_queue_exhausted value = 1 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_candidates value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_shortlisted value = 0 }
	every_owned_location = {
		if = { limit = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_candidates add = 1 } } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_local_score } root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_shortlisted add = 1 } } }
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_refresh_other_recommendations = {
    eu5ab_governor_refresh_begin = yes
    while = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
        count = 100000
        eu5ab_governor_refresh_tick = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase > 0 } eu5ab_governor_refresh_failed = yes }
}

eu5ab_governor_snapshot_transport = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_transport_schema value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier value = eu5ab_governor_highest_road_tier }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_global_speed value = modifier:global_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_land_speed value = modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_road_base value = modifier:road_cost_on_distance_from_capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_land_base value = modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_cached_lake_base value = modifier:sea_cost_on_distance_from_capital_when_maritime }
}

# New rules cannot resume an old worker phase or inherit its confirmation.
# Called from entry points and the worker, including while game time is paused.
eu5ab_governor_check_transport_rules = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_transport_schema } }
        eu5ab_governor_invalidate_transport_snapshot = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_transport_schema != 2 }
        eu5ab_governor_invalidate_transport_snapshot = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier } }
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier != eu5ab_governor_highest_road_tier }
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
    }
}

eu5ab_governor_invalidate_transport_snapshot = {
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_transport_resume_manual value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_transport_resume_manual value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } eu5ab_layout_abort = yes }
        if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_transport_resume_manual > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_transport_resume_manual
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_has_results
        eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = yes
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_result_road_tier
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_new_recommendation
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_notified_target
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1
        clear_variable_list = eu5ab_local_top_1_list
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_score
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_site_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_surrounding_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_1_coverage
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2
        clear_variable_list = eu5ab_local_top_2_list
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_score
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_site_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_surrounding_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_2_coverage
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3
        clear_variable_list = eu5ab_local_top_3_list
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_score
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_site_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_surrounding_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_top_3_coverage
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
}

# Multi-source baseline on exactly the network used for candidate footprints.
# This is deliberately not live proximity: projected road improvements are
# common to the existing and proposed layouts, never attributed to a new seat.
eu5ab_governor_prepare_planned_baseline = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_kind value = 3 }
    eu5ab_governor_prepare_planned_baseline_core = yes
}

# Naval candidates can traverse existing straits. Use the same connections in
# their baseline, without granting straits a hypothetical road.
eu5ab_governor_prepare_planned_naval_baseline = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_kind value = 2 }
    eu5ab_governor_prepare_planned_baseline_core = yes
}

eu5ab_governor_prepare_planned_baseline_core = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
    every_owned_location = {
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = 0 }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed value = modifier:local_proximity_source }
        if = {
            limit = { local_var:eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed max = 100 } }
            eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed
    }
    while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head }
        count = { value = num_locations multiply = 20 min = 100 }
        eu5ab_governor_queue_relax_one = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head }
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted
    }
    every_owned_location = {
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
        set_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_gov_node_demand
            value = { value = { value = modifier:local_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } subtract = var:eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
    }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
}

# Both manual buttons and the scheduled event call this same pipeline.
eu5ab_recalculate_governor_recommendations = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_run_layout
    eu5ab_governor_refresh_begin = yes
}

eu5ab_governor_initialize_relocation_setting = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { AND = { has_variable_map = cmm trigger_if = { limit = { has_variable_map = cmm } is_key_in_variable_map = { name = cmm target = flag:eu5ab_regional_development__governor_relocation_enabled } } } } }
		cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__governor_relocation_enabled value = 1 }
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_apply_migration_settings = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 } }
		eu5ab_governor_disable_migration = yes
		eu5ab_naval_planner_disable_migration = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_enabled > 0 } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } }
		eu5ab_governor_stop_auto_migration = yes
		eu5ab_naval_planner_stop_auto_migration = yes
	}
}

eu5ab_governor_stop_auto_migration = {
	# Stopping periodic work must not hide the last completed/manual result.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } }
		eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
		remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_failed
	}
	remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
}

eu5ab_governor_disable_migration = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } eu5ab_layout_abort = yes }
	remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_failed
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_has_results var:eu5ab_layout_result_count > 0 var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_count > 0 } }
		eu5ab_layout_clear_published_results = yes
	}
	remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
	eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = yes
	eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_migration = yes
}

eu5ab_layout_clear_published_results = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_has_results
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_result_proposal
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_result_road_tier
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_show_candidates
    eu5ab_layout_clear_candidates = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_count value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_before value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_after value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_net value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_kind value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_to
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_gain
    }
    eu5ab_layout_refresh_page = yes
}

eu5ab_layout_begin = {
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready } } eu5ab_governor_snapshot_local_slot_state = yes }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_generation } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_generation value = 0 } }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_generation add = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_work_generation value = var:eu5ab_layout_generation }
    # Snapshot the setting once; missing settings in older saves use the default.
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent value = 80 }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent min = 0 max = 200 } }
    }
    set_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_failed
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_last_footprint
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_force_keep
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_origin
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_target
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_destination_gain
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_compare_single
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_only
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_net value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_candidates value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_comparisons value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_proposal_kind value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seats value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_round value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_completed value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_before value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_last_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_fallback_batches value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_propagations value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_count value = num_locations }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_capital value = capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_capacity value = eu5ab_governor_relocation_capacity }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_occupied value = eu5ab_governor_relocation_occupied }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_wave_budget value = { value = num_locations multiply = 20 min = 100 } }

    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_total_value value = 0 }
        eu5ab_governor_snapshot_locations = yes
    }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 12 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
    eu5ab_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
    eu5ab_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
}

# A GUI pulse performs at most 64 queue or reduction operations.
# No game building is changed. All locations below belong to the snapshot country.
eu5ab_layout_reset_wave = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation add = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_footprint_nodes value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_wave_work value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 1 }
}

eu5ab_layout_visit = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = 0 }
    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_visited
    root = {
        eu5ab_governor_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_layout_visited }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_footprint_nodes add = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_seed = {
    if = {
        limit = { local_var:eu5ab_layout_seed_value > 0 }
        eu5ab_layout_visit = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_seed_value }
        eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_relax_one = {
    var:eu5ab_gov_queue_head = {
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_edge_from
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next }
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_head value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_queue_next } }
        }
        else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = {
                is_ownable = yes
                owner ?= root
                NOT = { eu5ab_is_strait_connection_to = { target = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from } }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
                eu5ab_layout_visit = yes
            }
            set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_relaxed value = eu5ab_governor_actual_land_edge_prediction }
            if = {
                limit = { local_var:eu5ab_layout_relaxed > var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_relaxed }
                eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
            }
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_relaxed
        }

        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = { OR = { topography = lakes topography = high_lakes topography = salt_pans } }
            every_neighbor_location = {
                limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { this = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from }  }
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation } var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation } }
                    eu5ab_layout_visit = yes
                }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed value = eu5ab_governor_lake_transit_prediction }
                if = {
                    limit = { local_var:eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed > var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed }

                    eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
                }
                remove_local_variable = eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed
            }
        }

    }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_wave_work add = 1 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 }
}

eu5ab_layout_collect_one = {
    if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 1 }
        # A high seed transforms propagation into shortest transport cost.
        # The watchdog rejects unfinished waves instead of truncating distance.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_distance value = { value = 1000000 subtract = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_band value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_distance divide = 40 floor = yes } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_band_order value = { value = 0 subtract = var:eu5ab_layout_band } }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 2 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_base value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_virtual value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 3 }
        root = {
            change_variable = {
                name = eu5ab_layout_before
                add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_predicted subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_base min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_current value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 5 }
        # Keep two distinct old sources. Ties give zero exclusive contribution.
        if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_predicted > var:eu5ab_layout_best_governor }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_second_governor value = var:eu5ab_layout_best_governor }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_best_governor value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_best_origin value = root.var:eu5ab_layout_source_origin }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_predicted > var:eu5ab_layout_second_governor }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_second_governor value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 6 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_retained value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 7 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_virtual value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
    }
    else = {
        root = {
            change_variable = {
                name = eu5ab_layout_evaluated_gain
                add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_predicted subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_virtual min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_compare_single } }
            eu5ab_layout_collect_single_gain = yes
        }
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

eu5ab_layout_start_source_wave = {
    eu5ab_layout_reset_wave = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 11 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
}

eu5ab_layout_wave_finished = {
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 2 }
        eu5ab_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 2 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 3 }
        eu5ab_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 3 }
        eu5ab_layout_next_old_source = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 5 }
        eu5ab_layout_next_old_source = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 6 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_after value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = num_locations }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 9 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 7 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 14 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_snapshot
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_after value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_planned_vs_live_difference value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
    else = {
        var:eu5ab_layout_candidate = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_upper_gain value = root.var:eu5ab_layout_evaluated_gain }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_scored_round value = root.var:eu5ab_layout_round }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_last_footprint value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 3 }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_force_keep }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = var:eu5ab_layout_footprint_nodes }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_head }
        }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_only } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 7 } }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_compare_single }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_resume_phase value = var:eu5ab_layout_phase }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_pending value = var:eu5ab_layout_seats }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 8 }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_evaluate_contender = {
    eu5ab_layout_reset_wave = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_evaluated_gain value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_compare_single
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_common value = 0 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_propagations add = 1 }
    var:eu5ab_layout_candidate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_checked } }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_compare_single }
        }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value value = var:eu5ab_layout_source }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value subtract = 80 } }
        change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value add = 80 }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_seed_value max = 100 } }
        eu5ab_layout_seed = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_seed_value
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_choose = {
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_round > var:eu5ab_layout_seats }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 7 }
    }
    else = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate
        ordered_owned_location = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_eligible NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected } var:eu5ab_layout_upper_gain >= root.var:eu5ab_layout_min_gain }
            order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_band_order
            max = 1
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_inner_band value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_band } }
        }
        ordered_owned_location = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = eu5ab_layout_eligible
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected }
                var:eu5ab_layout_band = root.var:eu5ab_layout_inner_band
                var:eu5ab_layout_upper_gain >= root.var:eu5ab_layout_min_gain
            }
            order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_upper_gain
            max = 1
            save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_contender
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_layout_contender } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    var:eu5ab_layout_candidate = { var:eu5ab_layout_scored_round = root.var:eu5ab_layout_round }
                    var:eu5ab_layout_last_footprint ?= var:eu5ab_layout_candidate
                }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = var:eu5ab_layout_footprint_nodes }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_head }
            }
            else = { eu5ab_layout_evaluate_contender = yes }
        }
        else = {
            # No remaining site clears the initial-build threshold. Retain an
            # old site, including its real (possibly small) modeled coverage.
            ordered_owned_location = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected } }
                order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_band_order
                max = 1
                save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_keep
                root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_layout_keep } }
            }
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_force_keep
            eu5ab_layout_evaluate_contender = yes
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_apply_one = {
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_predicted > var:eu5ab_layout_virtual }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_virtual value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

eu5ab_layout_abort = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_run_layout
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual
    eu5ab_layout_clear_liveness = yes
    set_variable = eu5ab_layout_failed
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
    every_in_list = {
        variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
    }
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
    # Keep previously published rows; this incomplete attempt isn't confirmation.
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
    remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration
    cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
}

eu5ab_layout_tick = {
    eu5ab_governor_check_transport_rules = yes
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
        eu5ab_governor_refresh_tick = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } }
        eu5ab_layout_recover_transport_restart = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_ticks add = 1 }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { num_locations != var:eu5ab_layout_snapshot_count capital != var:eu5ab_layout_snapshot_capital } }
            eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 } }
            eu5ab_governor_disable_migration = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_enabled > 0 } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } }
            eu5ab_governor_stop_auto_migration = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled <= 0 NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } }
            eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier > 0 }
                while = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head var:eu5ab_layout_wave_work < var:eu5ab_layout_wave_budget }
                count = 64
                eu5ab_layout_relax_one_planned = yes
            }
            }
            else = { while = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head var:eu5ab_layout_wave_work < var:eu5ab_layout_wave_budget }
                count = 64
                eu5ab_layout_relax_one = yes
            } }
            if = {
                limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head } }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = var:eu5ab_layout_footprint_nodes }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_head }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_wave_work >= var:eu5ab_layout_wave_budget }
                eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 2 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_layout_collect_one = yes
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes = 0 } eu5ab_layout_wave_finished = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 3 }
            eu5ab_layout_choose = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 4 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_layout_apply_one = yes
        }
    }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes = 0 }
                var:eu5ab_layout_candidate = {
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_selected value = root.var:eu5ab_layout_round }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_planning_gain value = var:eu5ab_layout_upper_gain }
                }
                change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_round add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_completed add = 1 }
                remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_force_keep
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 3 }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 5 }
            eu5ab_layout_publish = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 6 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_layout_collect_exclusive = yes
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes = 0 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 3 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 7 }
            eu5ab_layout_find_unchecked_candidate = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 8 }
            ordered_owned_location = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old var:eu5ab_layout_compared_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
                order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_band_order
                max = 64
                check_range_bounds = no
                eu5ab_layout_compare_old_seat = yes
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_single_pending = 0 }
                var:eu5ab_layout_candidate = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_checked }
                change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_candidates add = 1 }
                remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_compare_single
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = var:eu5ab_layout_resume_phase }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 9 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_layout_reduce_full_plan = yes
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes = 0 } eu5ab_layout_select_proposal = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 10 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_layout_prepare_single_winner = yes
        }
    }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes = 0 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 7 }
                eu5ab_layout_start_source_wave = yes
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 11 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }

        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value value = var:eu5ab_layout_source }
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old
                OR = {
                    root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 2
                    AND = {
                        OR = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 6 root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 7 }
                        NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected }
                    }
                }
            }
            change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value subtract = 80 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_mode = 7 has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } }
            change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value add = 80 }
        }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_seed_value min = 0 max = 100 } }
        eu5ab_layout_seed = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_seed_value
     root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 1 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 12 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
if = { limit = { owner ?= root }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_preview_from
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_preview_net
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_preview_before

        if = { limit = { root = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready } } }
            eu5ab_governor_clear_location_cache = yes
            eu5ab_governor_prepare_location_value = yes
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_source value = eu5ab_governor_current_proximity_source }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_native_source value = modifier:local_proximity_source }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = -1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_scored_round value = -1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_source value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_source }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_distance value = 1000000 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_band value = 25000 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_band_order value = -25000 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_base value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_virtual value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_current value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_best_governor value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_second_governor value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_exclusive value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_retained value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_recovery_generation value = -1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_compared_generation value = -1 }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_best_origin
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_old_wave_done
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_checked
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_old
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_eligible
        if = {
            limit = { has_building = building_type:local_governor }
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_old
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_eligible
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seats add = 1 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes }
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_eligible
        }
     } else = { root = { eu5ab_layout_abort = yes } } root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } } set_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 13 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 13 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }

        # An admissible upper bound; no live-Proximity shortlist can exclude a site.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_upper_gain value = root.var:eu5ab_governor_total_value }
     root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_min_gain value = eu5ab_governor_layout_candidate_threshold }
    eu5ab_layout_reset_wave = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 1 }
    capital = {
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value value = 1000000 }
        eu5ab_layout_seed = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_seed_value
    }
    eu5ab_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
    eu5ab_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 14 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    NOT = { owner ?= root }
                    var:eu5ab_layout_source != eu5ab_governor_current_proximity_source
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:local_governor } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } has_building = building_type:local_governor }
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } NOT = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes } }
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_snapshot }
        }
     root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 15 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_phase = 15 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } }

            root = {
                change_variable = {
                    name = eu5ab_layout_after
                    add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_virtual subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_base min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
                }
            }
            set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_live_difference value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_current subtract = var:eu5ab_gov_cached_old_proximity } }
            if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_layout_live_difference < 0 } change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_live_difference multiply = -1 } }
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_planned_vs_live_difference add = { value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_live_difference divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value } } }
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_live_difference
         root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 5 } }
        }
        else = { eu5ab_layout_abort = yes }
    }
}

# One pending event per country survives save/load and manual restarts.
eu5ab_layout_schedule_watchdog = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_human = yes
            has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
            NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_scheduled }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_scheduled
        trigger_event_silently = { id = eu5ab_governor.2 days = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_watchdog = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_scheduled
    if = {
        limit = { is_human = yes has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_ticks }
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_last_ticks }
                    var:eu5ab_layout_ticks = var:eu5ab_layout_watchdog_last_ticks
                }
            }
            # One bounded tick per fallback event; GUI and manual use the same work.
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_fallback_batches add = 1 }
            while = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
                count = 1
                eu5ab_layout_tick = yes
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_last_ticks value = var:eu5ab_layout_ticks }
        eu5ab_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_relax_one_planned = {
    var:eu5ab_gov_queue_head = {
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_edge_from
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next }
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_queue_head value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_queue_next } }
        }
        else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
        set_local_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_layout_source_prediction
            value = { value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted subtract = var:eu5ab_gov_planned_road_cost min = 0 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { local_var:eu5ab_layout_source_prediction > 0 }
            every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = {
                is_ownable = yes
                owner ?= root
                NOT = { eu5ab_is_strait_connection_to = { target = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from } }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
                eu5ab_layout_visit = yes
            }
            if = {
                limit = { local_var:eu5ab_layout_source_prediction > var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_source_prediction }
                eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
            }
        }
        }

        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = { OR = { topography = lakes topography = high_lakes topography = salt_pans } }
            every_neighbor_location = {
                limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { this = scope:eu5ab_gov_edge_from }  }
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation } var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation } }
                    eu5ab_layout_visit = yes
                }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed value = eu5ab_governor_lake_transit_prediction }
                if = {
                    limit = { local_var:eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed > var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed }

                    eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
                }
                remove_local_variable = eu5ab_gov_lake_relaxed
            }
        }

    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_source_prediction
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_wave_work add = 1 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work add = 1 }
}
# One wave per old source builds its exclusive contribution. Each new
# candidate footprint then evaluates all single replacements without repropagating
# the retained empire. All point and old-seat reductions run in bounded batches.
eu5ab_layout_recover_transport_restart = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual }
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
        remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_human = yes
            has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
            OR = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run }
        }
        # Recovery must not turn an automatic job into a manual override.
        if = {
