            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_recalculation_scheduled }
            set_variable = eu5ab_governor_skip_next_scheduled_event
        }
        eu5ab_governor_run_recalculation_now = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_next_old_source = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_source_origin
    ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old_wave_done } }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_band_order
        max = 1
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_old_wave_done
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_old_source
        root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_source_origin value = scope:eu5ab_layout_old_source } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_source_origin }
        eu5ab_layout_reset_wave = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 5 }
        var:eu5ab_layout_source_origin = {
            # Preserve other source buildings sharing this old seat.
            set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_seed_value value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_source min = 0 max = 100 } }
            eu5ab_layout_seed = yes
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_seed_value
        }
    }
    else = {
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = num_locations }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 6 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_collect_exclusive = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_best_origin }
        set_local_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_layout_exclusive_gain
            value = {
                value = var:eu5ab_layout_current
                subtract = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_base min = var:eu5ab_layout_second_governor }
                min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_gov_node_value
            }
        }
        var:eu5ab_layout_best_origin = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_exclusive add = local_var:eu5ab_layout_exclusive_gain }
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_exclusive_gain
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

eu5ab_layout_collect_single_gain = {
    set_local_variable = {
        name = eu5ab_layout_common_gain
        value = { value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted subtract = var:eu5ab_layout_current min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_common add = local_var:eu5ab_layout_common_gain } }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_best_origin }
        set_local_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_layout_recovery_gain
            value = {
                value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted
                subtract = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_base min = var:eu5ab_layout_second_governor }
                min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_gov_node_value
                subtract = local_var:eu5ab_layout_common_gain
            }
        }
        var:eu5ab_layout_best_origin = {
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_recovery_generation != root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_recovery_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_recovery value = 0 }
            }
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_recovery add = local_var:eu5ab_layout_recovery_gain }
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_recovery_gain
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_common_gain
}

eu5ab_layout_compare_old_seat = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_compared_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_swap_net value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_layout_single_common subtract = var:eu5ab_layout_exclusive } }
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_recovery_generation = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
        change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_swap_net add = var:eu5ab_layout_recovery }
    }
    # Save the best origin for every distinct destination, even below the gate.
    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_preview_origin
    root = {
        var:eu5ab_layout_candidate = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_preview_net }
                        local_var:eu5ab_layout_swap_net > var:eu5ab_layout_preview_net
                    }
                }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_preview_from value = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_origin }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_preview_before value = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_origin.var:eu5ab_layout_exclusive }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_preview_net value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_swap_net }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            local_var:eu5ab_layout_swap_net > root.var:eu5ab_layout_single_net
            local_var:eu5ab_layout_swap_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_layout_exclusive multiply = root.var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 }
        }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_swap_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_net value = local_var:eu5ab_layout_swap_net }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_origin value = scope:eu5ab_layout_swap_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_target value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_swap_net
    root = {
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_pending subtract = 1 }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_comparisons add = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_find_unchecked_candidate = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_seats > 0 }
        ordered_owned_location = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old }
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_checked }
                eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes
            }
            order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_band_order
            max = 1
            save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_unchecked_candidate
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_layout_unchecked_candidate } }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_only
        eu5ab_layout_evaluate_contender = yes
    }
    else = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_only
        # Full-plan threshold excludes the contribution of retained old seats.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 6 }
        eu5ab_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_reduce_full_plan = {
    root = {
        change_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_layout_after
            add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_virtual subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_base min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution
            add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_current subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_retained min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
        }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_select_proposal = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_net value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_after subtract = var:eu5ab_layout_before } }
    # Quantize the plan comparison to 0.00001 point for stable ties. This is
    # our deterministic comparison policy, not an assumption about the engine.
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_advantage value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_single_net subtract = var:eu5ab_layout_net multiply = 100000 round = yes } }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_full_qualifies
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:eu5ab_layout_net > 0
            var:eu5ab_layout_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution multiply = var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_full_qualifies
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_target
            OR = {
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_full_qualifies }
                local_var:eu5ab_layout_single_advantage > 0
            }
        }
        # Ties retain the qualifying full plan. Recompute winning coverage so
        # published scores and rows refer to exactly the same source set.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_proposal_kind value = 2 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution value = var:eu5ab_layout_single_origin.var:eu5ab_layout_exclusive }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_destination_gain value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_single_net add = var:eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = num_locations }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 10 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
    else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 14 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_snapshot
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_after value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_planned_vs_live_difference value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head } }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_advantage
}

eu5ab_layout_prepare_single_winner = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_virtual value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_planning_gain
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old this != root.var:eu5ab_layout_single_origin }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { this = root.var:eu5ab_layout_single_target }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

# Called only after every source wave and every planning round completed.
eu5ab_layout_publish = {
    eu5ab_governor_check_transport_rules = yes
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } }
        eu5ab_layout_recover_transport_restart = yes
    }
    else = {
    eu5ab_governor_snapshot_local_slot_state = yes
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                num_locations != var:eu5ab_layout_snapshot_count
                capital != var:eu5ab_layout_snapshot_capital
                var:eu5ab_layout_snapshot_capacity != eu5ab_governor_relocation_capacity
                var:eu5ab_layout_snapshot_occupied != eu5ab_governor_relocation_occupied
                var:eu5ab_layout_work_generation != var:eu5ab_layout_generation
            }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_snapshot
    }

    # The detailed pass can span pulses. Recheck ownership/native sources and
    # selected seats atomically with publication, without recalculating scores.
    every_in_list = {
        variable = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_locations
        if = {
            limit = { OR = {
                NOT = { owner ?= root }
                var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_native_source != modifier:local_proximity_source
                AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:local_governor } }
                AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } has_building = building_type:local_governor }
                AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } NOT = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes } }
            } }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_snapshot }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_snapshot }
        eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        # Demolishing one seat cannot free a replacement slot when already over cap.
        limit = { eu5ab_governor_relocation_occupied > eu5ab_governor_relocation_capacity }
        eu5ab_layout_clear_published_results = yes
        eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = yes
        remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
        remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready
        eu5ab_layout_clear_liveness = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty }
        # Dirty state belongs to both recommendation consumers. Refresh the
        # synchronous cards before layout_begin consumes it for the next plan.
        eu5ab_recalculate_governor_recommendations = yes
    }
    else = {

        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_net value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_after subtract = var:eu5ab_layout_before } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies
        if = {
            limit = {
                var:eu5ab_layout_net > 0
                var:eu5ab_layout_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_layout_changed_contribution multiply = var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 }
            }
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies
        }
        eu5ab_layout_publish_rows = yes
        eu5ab_layout_validate_rows = yes
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows }
            # Never combine a complete score/alert with a partial or mismatched list.
            eu5ab_layout_clear_published_results = yes
            eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
        }
        else = {
            eu5ab_layout_confirm = yes
            # UI reads only these published values, never work-in-progress fields.
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_before value = var:eu5ab_layout_before }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_road_tier value = var:eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_after value = var:eu5ab_layout_before }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_net value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_kind value = 0 }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_result_proposal
            if = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies }
                set_variable = eu5ab_layout_result_proposal
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_after value = var:eu5ab_layout_after }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_net value = var:eu5ab_layout_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_kind value = var:eu5ab_layout_proposal_kind }
            }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_has_results
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_result_count > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_has_results }
            eu5ab_layout_refresh_page = yes
            eu5ab_layout_publish_candidates = yes
            if = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run has_variable = eu5ab_layout_has_results }
                set_variable = eu5ab_layout_show_candidates
                remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_show_new
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_last_run_year value = current_year }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh value = 0 }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
            remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
            remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_ready
            eu5ab_layout_clear_liveness = yes
        }
    }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_confirm = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_changed
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_seats } var:eu5ab_layout_previous_seats != var:eu5ab_layout_seats } }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_changed
    }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_previous_seats value = var:eu5ab_layout_seats }
    every_owned_location = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_target } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected } has_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_target }
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_old } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } has_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_old }
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_changed }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_target
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_old
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_target }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_old }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
    }
    else_if = {
        # An explicit request publishes a qualifying plan immediately. The
        # confirmation/cooldown rules below apply only to unsolicited alerts.
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
        set_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
        cmf_show_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_changed NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak } } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak value = 1 }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration
            cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
        }
        else = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak add = 1 } }
        if = {
            limit = {
                var:eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak >= 2
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration }
                OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_local_migration_cooldown } var:eu5ab_local_migration_cooldown <= 0 }
            }
            set_variable = eu5ab_local_has_migration
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_migration_cooldown value = 12 }
            cmf_show_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_relocation_alert }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_publish_rows = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_count value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_to
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_matched
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_display
        if = {
            limit = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies } has_variable = eu5ab_layout_selected }
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_display
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { root = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies } } has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old }
            set_variable = eu5ab_layout_display
        }
        # Reserve retained origins before matching ANY new destination.
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old has_variable = eu5ab_layout_display } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_matched }
    }
    ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_display }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_band_order
        # Ordered effects default to one item, not the whole filtered list.
        max = var:eu5ab_layout_seats
        check_range_bounds = no
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_row_destination
        root = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_count add = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { scope:eu5ab_layout_row_destination = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old } }
                scope:eu5ab_layout_row_destination = { eu5ab_layout_store_row = yes }
            }
            else = {
                every_owned_location = {
                    limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_matched } }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_match_score value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_distance subtract = scope:eu5ab_layout_row_destination.var:eu5ab_layout_distance } }
                    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_match_score > 0 } change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_match_score multiply = -1 } }
                }
                ordered_owned_location = {
                    limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_matched } }
                    order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_match_score
                    max = 1
                    eu5ab_layout_store_row = yes
                }
            }
        }
    }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page value = 0 }
}

eu5ab_layout_store_row = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_layout_matched
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_row_order value = root.var:eu5ab_layout_result_count }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_row_to value = scope:eu5ab_layout_row_destination }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_row_gain value = 0 }
    if = {
        limit = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies } }
        if = {
            limit = { root.var:eu5ab_layout_proposal_kind = 2 }
            # Single swaps have a conditional gain, not a greedy-round gain.
            remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_gain
        }
        else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_row_gain value = scope:eu5ab_layout_row_destination.var:eu5ab_layout_planning_gain } }
    }
}

# Validate the one-to-one origin/destination mapping before publishing scores
# or showing an alert. Counting the destinations alone misses failed matches.
eu5ab_layout_validate_rows = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_display_count value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_stored_rows value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_validated_moves value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_destination_seen
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_display }
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_display_count add = 1 } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old
                OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_to } }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows }
        }
    }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order }
        root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_stored_rows add = 1 } }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_old }
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_to }
                    var:eu5ab_layout_row_order < 1
                    var:eu5ab_layout_row_order > root.var:eu5ab_layout_result_count
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            if = {
                limit = { this != var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
                root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_validated_moves add = 1 } }
            }
            var:eu5ab_layout_row_to = {
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = { NOT = { owner ?= root } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_display } has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_destination_seen }
                    }
                    root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows }
                }
                set_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_destination_seen
            }
        }
    }
    every_owned_location = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_display NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_destination_seen } }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_destination_seen
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                var:eu5ab_layout_result_count != var:eu5ab_layout_seats
                var:eu5ab_layout_display_count != var:eu5ab_layout_seats
                var:eu5ab_layout_stored_rows != var:eu5ab_layout_seats
                AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies var:eu5ab_layout_validated_moves = 0 }
                AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_qualifies } var:eu5ab_layout_validated_moves > 0 }
            }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_layout_invalid_rows
    }
}

eu5ab_layout_refresh_page = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page_count value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_result_count divide = 3 ceiling = yes min = 1 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_page min = 0 max = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_page_count subtract = 1 } } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page_number value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_page add = 1 } }
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_view_1_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_1_retained
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_1_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_1_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_1_order
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page_row value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_page multiply = 3 add = 1 } }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order var:eu5ab_layout_row_order = local_var:eu5ab_layout_page_row }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_page_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_1_from value = scope:eu5ab_layout_page_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_1_to value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_1_order value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_order }
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_1_list target = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_view_1_from = var:eu5ab_layout_view_1_to } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_1_retained }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_page_row
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_view_2_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_2_retained
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_2_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_2_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_2_order
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page_row value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_page multiply = 3 add = 2 } }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order var:eu5ab_layout_row_order = local_var:eu5ab_layout_page_row }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_page_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_2_from value = scope:eu5ab_layout_page_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_2_to value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_2_order value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_order }
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_2_list target = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_view_2_from = var:eu5ab_layout_view_2_to } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_2_retained }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_page_row
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_view_3_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_3_retained
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_3_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_3_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_3_order
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_page_row value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_page multiply = 3 add = 3 } }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_row_order var:eu5ab_layout_row_order = local_var:eu5ab_layout_page_row }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_page_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_3_from value = scope:eu5ab_layout_page_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_3_to value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_3_order value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_order }
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_view_3_list target = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_row_to }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_view_3_from = var:eu5ab_layout_view_3_to } set_variable = eu5ab_layout_view_3_retained }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_layout_page_row
}

eu5ab_layout_clear_candidates = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_count value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_result_threshold_percent
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_before
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_after
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_net
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_percent
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_qualifies
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_zero_base
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_before
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_after
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_net
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_percent
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_qualifies
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_zero_base
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_before
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_after
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_net
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_percent
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_qualifies
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_zero_base
}

eu5ab_layout_publish_candidates = {
    eu5ab_layout_clear_candidates = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_result_threshold_percent value = var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent }
    ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_preview_from eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_layout_preview_net
        max = 3
        check_range_bounds = no
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_layout_preview_target
        root = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_count add = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_count = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_from value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_from }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_to value = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_target }
                add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_list target = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_target }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_before value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_before }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_net value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_after value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_before add = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_net } }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_before > 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_net divide = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_before multiply = 100 } }
                }
                else = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_zero_base }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_net > 0 var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_before multiply = var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 } }
                    set_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_1_qualifies
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_count = 2 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_from value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_from }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_to value = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_target }
                add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_list target = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_target }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_before value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_before }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_net value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_after value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_before add = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_net } }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_before > 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_net divide = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_before multiply = 100 } }
                }
                else = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_zero_base }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_net > 0 var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_before multiply = var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 } }
                    set_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_2_qualifies
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_count = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_from value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_from }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_to value = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_target }
                add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_list target = scope:eu5ab_layout_preview_target }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_before value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_before }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_net value = prev.var:eu5ab_layout_preview_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_after value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_before add = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_net } }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_before > 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_net divide = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_before multiply = 100 } }
                }
                else = { set_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_zero_base }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_net > 0 var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_before multiply = var:eu5ab_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 } }
                    set_variable = eu5ab_layout_candidate_3_qualifies
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_governor_initialize_threshold_setting = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { AND = { has_variable_map = cmm trigger_if = { limit = { has_variable_map = cmm } is_key_in_variable_map = { name = cmm target = flag:eu5ab_regional_development__governor_migration_threshold_percent } } } } }
        cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__governor_migration_threshold_percent value = 80 }
    }
}

# CMM supplies +/-1 and Ctrl +/-5 natively. Shift writes an endpoint before
# the callback; convert only this numeric setting's endpoint to a +/-10 step.
# Normal/Ctrl clicks that reach a bound produce the same clamped result.
# Reset writes 80, not an endpoint, so it is never reinterpreted.
eu5ab_governor_adjust_threshold_step = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent }
        if = {
            limit = {
                "variable_map(cmm|flag:eu5ab_regional_development__governor_migration_threshold_percent)" = 200
                var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent < 200
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step value = { value = var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent add = 10 max = 200 } }
            cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__governor_migration_threshold_percent value = var:eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                "variable_map(cmm|flag:eu5ab_regional_development__governor_migration_threshold_percent)" = 0
                var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent > 0
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step value = { value = var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_threshold_percent subtract = 10 min = 0 } }
            cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__governor_migration_threshold_percent value = var:eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step }
        }
    }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step
}

# Manual refresh drains the same planner without GUI/date delays.
# A malformed job must terminate explicitly instead of leaving a pending task.
eu5ab_layout_finish_manual = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
        while = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
            count = 100000
            eu5ab_layout_tick = yes
            eu5ab_layout_check_liveness = yes
        }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } eu5ab_layout_abort = yes }
    }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } } remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run }
}

eu5ab_layout_clear_liveness = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_liveness_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_stalled_checks
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_0
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_1
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_2
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_3
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_4
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_5
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_6
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_7
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_8
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_9
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_observed_10
}

eu5ab_layout_snapshot_progress = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_layout_liveness_ready
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_stalled_checks value = 0 }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_phase } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_phase value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_0 value = var:eu5ab_layout_phase }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_mode } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_mode value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_1 value = var:eu5ab_layout_mode }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_round } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_round value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_2 value = var:eu5ab_layout_round }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_3 value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_total_work } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_total_work value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_4 value = var:eu5ab_layout_total_work }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_5 value = var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_completed } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_completed value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_6 value = var:eu5ab_layout_completed }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_candidates } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_candidates value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_7 value = var:eu5ab_layout_single_candidates }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_single_comparisons } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_single_comparisons value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_8 value = var:eu5ab_layout_single_comparisons }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_9 value = var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_work } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_work value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_observed_10 value = var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_work }
}

eu5ab_layout_check_liveness = {
    if = {
        limit = { is_human = yes has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active }
        if = {
            # Old saves have no observation. Give them a full observation window.
            limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_layout_liveness_ready } }
            eu5ab_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { OR = {
                var:eu5ab_layout_phase != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_0
                var:eu5ab_layout_mode != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_1
                var:eu5ab_layout_round != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_2
                var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_3
                var:eu5ab_layout_total_work != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_4
                var:eu5ab_layout_pending_nodes != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_5
                var:eu5ab_layout_completed != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_6
                var:eu5ab_layout_single_candidates != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_7
                var:eu5ab_layout_single_comparisons != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_8
                var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_9
                var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_work != var:eu5ab_layout_observed_10
            } }
            eu5ab_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
        }
        else = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_stalled_checks add = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_layout_stalled_checks >= 2 }
                eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
            }
        }
    }
}


# A refresh publishes only after validation. Previous cards survive partial work.
eu5ab_governor_refresh_begin = {
    eu5ab_governor_snapshot_transport = yes

	# All four native types have one level. In-progress buildings occupy slots
	# but do not add a completed source. Keep raw counts for diagnostics.
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_capacity value = effective_num_local_governors }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_lieutenancies value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_itinerant_seats value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_kings_manors value = 0 }

    set_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_new
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_layout
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed
    remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted
    remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_failed
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_sync_runs } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_runs value = 0 } }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_runs add = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_total_value value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_work value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_candidates value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_seats value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_snapshot_count value = num_locations }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_snapshot_capital value = capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_watchdog_last_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_fallback_batches value = 0 }
    eu5ab_governor_snapshot_locations = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
    eu5ab_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
    eu5ab_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
}

# Only lightweight links are built synchronously; expensive per-location work
# advances through the saved cursor without repeatedly sorting the whole realm.
eu5ab_governor_snapshot_locations = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_tail
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_locations
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_node
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_locations target = scope:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_node }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_tail }
                var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next value = scope:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_node } }
            }
            else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head value = scope:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_node } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_tail value = scope:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_node }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_governor_append_candidate = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_next
    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_new_candidate
    root = {
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_tail }
            var:eu5ab_gov_candidate_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_candidate_next value = scope:eu5ab_gov_new_candidate } }
        }
        else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_candidate_head value = scope:eu5ab_gov_new_candidate } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_candidate_tail value = scope:eu5ab_gov_new_candidate }
    }
}

eu5ab_governor_refresh_tick = {
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_layout_ticks add = 1 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_work add = 1 }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled <= 0 NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } }
        eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 1 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }

		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:local_governor any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:local_governor building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:lieutenancy any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:lieutenancy building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_lieutenancies add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:itinerant_court_seat any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:itinerant_court_seat building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_itinerant_seats add = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:kings_manor any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:kings_manor building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
			root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_fresh_kings_manors add = 1 } }
		}

        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_cheap_score
        remove_variable = eu5ab_local_score
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_old
        if = { limit = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes }
            set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_candidates add = 1 } }
        }
        if = { limit = { has_building = building_type:local_governor }
            set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_old
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_seats add = 1 } }
        }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_other_slots value = eu5ab_governor_fresh_local_other_slots }
            if = { limit = { eu5ab_fresh_local_governor_slot_available = yes var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_candidates > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_new }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_seats > 0 var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 OR = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_enabled > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } } set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_layout }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 9 }
            if = { limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_new has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_layout } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 2 } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 2 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
if = { limit = { owner ?= root }
        eu5ab_governor_clear_location_cache = yes
        eu5ab_governor_prepare_location_value = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_native_source value = modifier:local_proximity_source }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_source value = eu5ab_governor_current_proximity_source }
     } else = { root = { set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid } }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
            set_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 9 }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_new NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid } }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_kind value = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
                remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
                remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
                clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_work value = 0 }
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 3 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }

        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = 0 }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_native_source }
        if = {
            limit = { local_var:eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed max = 100 } }
            eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_gov_baseline_seed

        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 4 } }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 4 }
        while = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } count = 32
            eu5ab_governor_queue_relax_one = yes
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_work add = 1 }
        }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 5 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
        else_if = { limit = { NOT = { eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } }
            set_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed
            eu5ab_governor_refresh_failed = yes
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 5 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }

        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity value = var:eu5ab_gov_predicted }
        set_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_gov_node_demand
            value = { value = { value = modifier:local_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } subtract = var:eu5ab_gov_planned_proximity min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
        remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next

        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
            clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
            remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_head
            remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_tail
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 6 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 6 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible } eu5ab_governor_prepare_local_cheap_score = yes }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
            ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_local_cheap_score
        max = 8
        check_range_bounds = no
        eu5ab_governor_append_candidate = yes
    }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 7 }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 7 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_head }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_kind value = 1 }
            var:eu5ab_gov_candidate_head = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
		clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation } }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
		}
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation add = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_value value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage value = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_predicted value = { value = eu5ab_governor_current_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_depth value = 0 }
	root = { eu5ab_governor_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate } }
	eu5ab_governor_queue_enqueue = yes
 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_work value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 8 }
        }
        else = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 9 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 8 }
        while = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } count = 32
            eu5ab_governor_queue_relax_one = yes
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_work add = 1 }
        }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 10 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_head }
        }
        else_if = { limit = { NOT = { eu5ab_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } } eu5ab_governor_refresh_failed = yes }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 10 }
        var:eu5ab_gov_candidate_head = { save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }

			if = {
				limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_visit_generation = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_generation }
				if = {
					limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_predicted > eu5ab_governor_comparison_proximity }
					if = {
						limit = { this ?= scope:eu5ab_gov_evaluated_candidate }
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain add = prev.eu5ab_governor_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					else = {
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain add = prev.eu5ab_governor_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_value add = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_node_value }
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage add = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_in_queue
			remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_next

        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }
            var:eu5ab_gov_candidate_head = {

	# Quantize each displayed component first so the one-decimal card values
	# add exactly to the displayed overall score.
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_gain
		value = { value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain add = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_result_coverage value = root.var:eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage }
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_queue_tail
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_site_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_surrounding_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_value
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_coverage
	}


        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_score value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_gain }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_site_gain value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_site_gain }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_surrounding_gain value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_local_coverage value = var:eu5ab_gov_eval_result_coverage }

                if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_candidate_head value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_candidate_next } } }
                else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_candidate_head } }
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 7 }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase = 9 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } }

        if = { limit = { OR = {
            NOT = { owner ?= root }
            AND = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready } var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_native_source != modifier:local_proximity_source }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:local_governor } }
            AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_old } has_building = building_type:local_governor }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible NOT = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes } }
            AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible } eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes }
        } } root = { set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid } }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_cursor } } eu5ab_governor_refresh_commit = yes }
    }
    else = { eu5ab_governor_refresh_failed = yes }
}

eu5ab_governor_refresh_failed = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 4 }
    eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
}

eu5ab_governor_refresh_commit = {

    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_capacity value = var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_capacity }
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_occupied value = var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied }
    eu5ab_governor_snapshot_local_slot_state = yes
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_capacity != local_var:eu5ab_refresh_capacity } set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_occupied != local_var:eu5ab_refresh_occupied } set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_other_slots != eu5ab_governor_fresh_local_other_slots } set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_occupied
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_capacity
    every_in_list = {
        variable = eu5ab_layout_snapshot_locations
        if = { limit = { OR = {
            NOT = { owner ?= root }
            AND = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready } var:eu5ab_gov_snapshot_native_source != modifier:local_proximity_source }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:local_governor } }
            AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_old } has_building = building_type:local_governor }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_eligible NOT = { eu5ab_local_governor_candidate = yes } }
        } } root = { set_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid } }
    }
    if = { limit = { OR = {
        has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_invalid
        has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
        num_locations != var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_snapshot_count
        capital != var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_snapshot_capital
        var:eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier != eu5ab_governor_highest_road_tier
        var:eu5ab_gov_cached_global_speed != modifier:global_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation
        var:eu5ab_gov_cached_land_speed != modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation
        var:eu5ab_gov_cached_road_base != modifier:road_cost_on_distance_from_capital
        var:eu5ab_gov_cached_land_base != modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital
        var:eu5ab_gov_cached_lake_base != modifier:sea_cost_on_distance_from_capital_when_maritime
    } }
        # Abort clears overrides; restore the manual intent explicitly below.
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_manual value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run } set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_manual value = 1 } }
        eu5ab_layout_abort = yes
        if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_refresh_manual > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual }
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
        eu5ab_layout_recover_transport_restart = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_manual
    }
    else = {
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_total value = var:eu5ab_governor_total_value }
        eu5ab_governor_clear_country_results = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_total_value value = local_var:eu5ab_refresh_total }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_total
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_new } eu5ab_governor_rank_local_results = yes }
		eu5ab_governor_snapshot_local_diagnostics = yes
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_stage value = 3 }
		# Preserve the completed road label before layout work resets scratch data.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted } }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_result_road_tier value = var:eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier }
		}

		eu5ab_governor_evaluate_local_alerts = yes
		# Published status is independent of the subsequent layout worker cache.
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { eu5ab_fresh_local_governor_slot_available = yes } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_sync_shortlisted > 0 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_local_has_new_recommendation } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 5 } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { var:eu5ab_gov_sync_baseline_failed > 0 var:eu5ab_gov_sync_queue_exhausted > 0 } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 4 } }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_stage value = 4 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_baseline_failed } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_queue_exhausted } }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_result_road_tier value = var:eu5ab_gov_transport_road_tier }
		}
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_last_run_year value = current_year }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_governor_months_since_refresh value = 0 }
		remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_kind
		remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_eval_generation

        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_new } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_gov_fresh_local_capacity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_sync_status value = 6 }
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_refresh_phase value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_run_layout has_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_need_layout }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_run_layout
            eu5ab_layout_begin = yes
        }
        else = {
            remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_refresh_run_layout
            remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active
            remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
            remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_inputs_ready
            eu5ab_layout_clear_liveness = yes
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:eu5ab_gov_refresh_seats = 0 NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 } } }
                eu5ab_layout_clear_published_results = yes
                eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = yes
            }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_governor_record_visited = {
    add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations target = $node$ }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail }
        var:eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_footprint_next value = $node$ } }
    }
    else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_footprint_head value = $node$ } }
    $node$ = { remove_variable = eu5ab_gov_footprint_next }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_gov_footprint_tail value = $node$ }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_location_cache = {
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_proximity
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_local_speed
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_topography
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_topography_impact
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_vegetation
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_node_demand
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_population
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_potential_tax_base
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_development
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_cost_factor
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_road_cost
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_road_edge_base_cost
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_land_edge_base_cost
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_proximity
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_control
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_max_control
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_prepare_location_value = {
	eu5ab_prepare_strait_connections = yes
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_local_speed value = modifier:local_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_topography value = eu5ab_naval_planner_topography_penalty }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_topography_impact value = eu5ab_naval_planner_topography_impact }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_vegetation value = eu5ab_naval_planner_vegetation_penalty }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_population value = population }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_potential_tax_base value = eu5ab_naval_planner_potential_tax_base }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_development value = development }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value value = eu5ab_naval_planner_cached_location_value }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_cost_factor value = eu5ab_naval_planner_edge_cost_factor }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_road_edge_base_cost value = eu5ab_naval_planner_road_edge_base_cost }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_road_cost value = eu5ab_naval_planner_planned_road_cost }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_land_edge_base_cost value = eu5ab_naval_planner_land_edge_base_cost }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_proximity value = proximity }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_control value = local_control }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_max_control value = max_control }
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_node_demand
		value = {
			value = 80
			subtract = proximity
			min = 0
			divide = 100
			multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value
		}
	}
	root = {
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_total_value add = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
	}
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_new
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue } }
		set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
		root = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail }
				var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_new } }
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_new }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_new }
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_new }
			}
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_relax_one = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head }
		var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head = { save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_current }
		scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_current = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next }
				var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next = { save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_next }
				root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_next } }
			}
			else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail } }
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_kind != 1 var:eu5ab_naval_gov_depth < 6 } var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted > 0 }
				save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from
				every_neighbor_location = {
					limit = {
						is_ownable = yes
						owner ?= root
						OR = {
							root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_kind = 2
							NOT = { eu5ab_is_strait_connection_to = { target = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from } }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation } }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = -1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = 0 }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = 99 }
						save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_newly_visited
						root = { eu5ab_naval_planner_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_newly_visited } }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_kind = 2 }
						set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_land_edge_prediction }
					}
					else = { set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_known_land_edge_prediction } }
					if = {
						limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_relaxed > var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_relaxed }
						set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = { value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_depth add = 1 } }
						eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
					}
					remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_relaxed
				}

        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = { OR = { topography = lakes topography = high_lakes topography = salt_pans } }
            every_neighbor_location = {
                limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { this = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from } OR = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_kind != 1 scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_depth <= 4 } }
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation } var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation } }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = 99 }
                    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_newly_visited
                    root = { eu5ab_naval_planner_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_newly_visited } }
                }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_lake_transit_prediction }
                if = {
                    limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed > var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
