                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = { value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_depth add = 2 } }
                    eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
                }
                remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed
            }
        }

			}
		}
	}
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_evaluate_candidate = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
		clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation } }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
		}
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation add = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_value value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage value = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = { value = eu5ab_naval_planner_current_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = 0 }
	root = { eu5ab_naval_planner_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate } }
	eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
	root = {
		while = {
			limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head }
			count = { value = num_locations multiply = 4 min = 100 }
			eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_relax_one = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_exhausted }
		every_in_list = {
			variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
			if = {
				limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
				if = {
					limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted > eu5ab_naval_planner_comparison_proximity }
					if = {
						limit = { this ?= scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate }
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain add = prev.eu5ab_naval_planner_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					else = {
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain add = prev.eu5ab_naval_planner_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_value add = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage add = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
		}
		clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
	}
	# Quantize each displayed component first so the one-decimal card values
	# add exactly to the displayed overall score.
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_site_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_gain
		value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_site_gain add = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_coverage value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage }
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_value
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage
	}
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_transport = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_schema value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier value = eu5ab_naval_planner_highest_road_tier }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_global_speed value = modifier:global_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_land_speed value = modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_road_base value = modifier:road_cost_on_distance_from_capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_land_base value = modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_lake_base value = modifier:sea_cost_on_distance_from_capital_when_maritime }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_prepare_planned_naval_baseline = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_kind value = 2 }
    eu5ab_naval_planner_prepare_planned_baseline_core = yes
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_prepare_planned_baseline_core = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_baseline_failed
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
    every_owned_location = {
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_proximity value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = 0 }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_baseline_seed value = modifier:local_proximity_source }
        if = {
            limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_baseline_seed > 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_baseline_seed max = 100 } }
            eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_baseline_seed
    }
    while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head }
        count = { value = num_locations multiply = 20 min = 100 }
        eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_relax_one = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_baseline_failed
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_exhausted
    }
    every_owned_location = {
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_proximity value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
        set_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_naval_gov_node_demand
            value = { value = { value = modifier:local_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_proximity min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
    }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_store_local_rank = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate
	root = {
		if = {
			limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_rank_cursor = 0 }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_1 value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_1_list target = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_1_score value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_score }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_1_site_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_site_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_1_surrounding_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_surrounding_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_1_coverage value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_coverage }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_rank_cursor = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_2 value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_2_list target = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_2_score value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_score }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_2_site_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_site_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_2_surrounding_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_surrounding_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_2_coverage value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_coverage }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_3 value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_3_list target = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_3_score value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_score }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_3_site_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_site_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_3_surrounding_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_surrounding_gain }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_top_3_coverage value = scope:eu5ab_naval_ranked_candidate.var:eu5ab_naval_coverage }
		}
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_rank_cursor add = 1 }
	}
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_rank_local_results = {
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_rank_cursor value = 0 }
	ordered_owned_location = {
		limit = {
			eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes
			has_variable = eu5ab_naval_score
			var:eu5ab_naval_score > 0
			root = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_exhausted } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_baseline_failed } }
		}
		order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_score
		max = 3
		check_range_bounds = no
		eu5ab_naval_planner_store_local_rank = yes
	}
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_rank_cursor
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_evaluate_local_alerts = {
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_new_recommendation
	if = {
		limit = { eu5ab_fresh_naval_governor_slot_available = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1
				var:eu5ab_naval_top_1_score >= eu5ab_naval_planner_new_recommendation_threshold
				# The vanilla can-build trigger requires scope:actor and is invalid in
				# this background country effect. Slot capacity is checked above; this
				# rechecks the actor-independent location conditions instead.
				var:eu5ab_naval_top_1 = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes }
			}
			set_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_new_recommendation
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_realert
						NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_notified_target }
						var:eu5ab_naval_notified_target != var:eu5ab_naval_top_1
					}
				}
				cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert }
				set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_notified_target value = var:eu5ab_naval_top_1 }
				cmf_show_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert }
			}
		}
		else = {
			cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert }
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_notified_target
		}
	}
	else = {
		cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert }
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_notified_target
		# Local migration is published by the complete-layout planner.
	}
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_naval_results = {
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_result_road_tier
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_new_recommendation
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_top_1_list
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_top_2_list
	clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_top_3_list
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_1_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_2_coverage
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_score
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_site_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_surrounding_gain
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_top_3_coverage
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_local_migration = {
	cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
	remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
}
eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_naval_slots = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_capacity value = modifier:num_naval_governors }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_occupied value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:naval_governor building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_occupied add = 1 } }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_other_recommendations = {
    eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_begin = yes
    while = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
        count = 100000
        eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_tick = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase > 0 } eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_failed = yes }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_recalculate = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_run_layout
    eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_begin = yes
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_run_recalculation_now = {
    if = {
        limit = { is_human = yes OR = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty }
        else = { eu5ab_naval_planner_recalculate = yes }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_recalculation = {
    if = {
        limit = { is_human = yes }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_realert


        eu5ab_naval_planner_recalculate = yes
        eu5ab_naval_layout_finish_manual = yes
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_realert
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_check_transport_rules = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_schema } }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_schema != 2 }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier } }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier != eu5ab_naval_planner_highest_road_tier }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_stop_auto_migration = {
    if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } } eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_failed }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_disable_migration = {
    if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_failed
    eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_published_results = yes
    eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_local_migration = yes
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_published_results = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_has_results
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_proposal
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_road_tier
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_show_candidates
    eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_candidates = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_before value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_after value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_net value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_kind value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_gain
    }
    eu5ab_naval_layout_refresh_page = yes
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_begin = {
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready } } eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_naval_slots = yes }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_generation } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_generation value = 0 } }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_generation add = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_work_generation value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_generation }
    # Snapshot the setting once; missing settings in older saves use the default.
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent value = 80 }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent min = 0 max = 200 } }
    }
    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_failed
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_last_footprint
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_force_keep
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_origin
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_target
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_destination_gain
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_single
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_only
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_net value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_candidates value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_comparisons value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_proposal_kind value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seats value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_round value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_completed value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_before value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_last_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_fallback_batches value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_propagations value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_count value = num_locations }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_capital value = capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_capacity value = eu5ab_naval_planner_relocation_capacity }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_occupied value = eu5ab_naval_planner_relocation_occupied }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_budget value = { value = num_locations multiply = 20 min = 100 } }

    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_total_value value = 0 }
        eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_locations = yes
    }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 12 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
    eu5ab_naval_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
    eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
}

# A GUI pulse performs at most 64 queue or reduction operations.
# No game building is changed. All locations below belong to the snapshot country.
eu5ab_naval_layout_reset_wave = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation add = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_footprint_nodes value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_work value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 1 }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_visit = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = 0 }
    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_visited
    root = {
        eu5ab_naval_planner_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_visited }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_footprint_nodes add = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_seed = {
    if = {
        limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value > 0 }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_visit = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value }
        eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_relax_one = {
    var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head = {
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next }
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next } }
        }
        else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = {
                is_ownable = yes
                owner ?= root

            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
                eu5ab_naval_layout_visit = yes
            }
            set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_actual_land_edge_prediction }
            if = {
                limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed > var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed }
                eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
            }
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed
        }

        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = { OR = { topography = lakes topography = high_lakes topography = salt_pans } }
            every_neighbor_location = {
                limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { this = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from }  }
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation } var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation } }
                    eu5ab_naval_layout_visit = yes
                }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_lake_transit_prediction }
                if = {
                    limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed > var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed }

                    eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
                }
                remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed
            }
        }

    }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_work add = 1 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_collect_one = {
    if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 1 }
        # A high seed transforms propagation into shortest transport cost.
        # The watchdog rejects unfinished waves instead of truncating distance.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_distance value = { value = 1000000 subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_band value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_distance divide = 40 floor = yes } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order value = { value = 0 subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band } }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 2 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_base value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 3 }
        root = {
            change_variable = {
                name = eu5ab_naval_layout_before
                add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_base min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_current value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 5 }
        # Keep two distinct old sources. Ties give zero exclusive contribution.
        if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted > var:eu5ab_naval_layout_best_governor }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_second_governor value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_best_governor }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_best_governor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_best_origin value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source_origin }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted > var:eu5ab_naval_layout_second_governor }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_second_governor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 6 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_retained value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 7 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
    }
    else = {
        root = {
            change_variable = {
                name = eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluated_gain
                add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_single } }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_collect_single_gain = yes
        }
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_start_source_wave = {
    eu5ab_naval_layout_reset_wave = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 11 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_finished = {
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 2 }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 2 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 3 }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 3 }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_next_old_source = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 5 }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_next_old_source = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 6 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_after value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = num_locations }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 9 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 7 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 14 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_snapshot
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_after value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_planned_vs_live_difference value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
    else = {
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluated_gain }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_scored_round value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_round }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_last_footprint value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 3 }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_force_keep }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_footprint_nodes }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head }
        }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_only } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 7 } }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_single }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_resume_phase value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_pending value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 8 }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluate_contender = {
    eu5ab_naval_layout_reset_wave = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluated_gain value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_single
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_common value = 0 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_propagations add = 1 }
    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_checked } }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_single }
        }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value subtract = 80 } }
        change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value add = 80 }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value max = 100 } }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_seed = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_choose = {
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_round > var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 7 }
    }
    else = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate
        ordered_owned_location = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_eligible NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected } var:eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain >= root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_min_gain }
            order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order
            max = 1
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_inner_band value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band } }
        }
        ordered_owned_location = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_eligible
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected }
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_inner_band
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain >= root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_min_gain
            }
            order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain
            max = 1
            save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_contender
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_contender } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate }
            var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = { set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_tied_gain value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain } }
            ordered_owned_location = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected } var:eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_tied_gain }
                order_by = 1
                max = 1
                save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_tied_old
                root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_tied_old } }
            }
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_tied_gain
            if = {
                limit = {
                    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_scored_round = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_round }
                    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_last_footprint ?= var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate
                }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_footprint_nodes }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head }
            }
            else = { eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluate_contender = yes }
        }
        else = {
            # No remaining site clears the initial-build threshold. Retain an
            # old site, including its real (possibly small) modeled coverage.
            ordered_owned_location = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected } }
                order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order
                max = 1
                save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_keep
                root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_keep } }
            }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_force_keep
            eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluate_contender = yes
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_apply_one = {
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted > var:eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_run_layout
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_resume_manual
    eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_liveness = yes
    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_failed
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
    every_in_list = {
        variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
    }
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
    # Keep previously published rows; this incomplete attempt isn't confirmation.
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
    cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_tick = {
    eu5ab_naval_planner_check_transport_rules = yes
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
        eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_tick = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_recover_transport_restart = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks add = 1 }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { num_locations != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_count capital != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_capital } }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 } }
            eu5ab_naval_planner_disable_migration = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_enabled > 0 } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } }
            eu5ab_naval_planner_stop_auto_migration = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled <= 0 NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier > 0 }
                while = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head var:eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_work < var:eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_budget }
                count = 64
                eu5ab_naval_layout_relax_one_planned = yes
            }
            }
            else = { while = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head var:eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_work < var:eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_budget }
                count = 64
                eu5ab_naval_layout_relax_one = yes
            } }
            if = {
                limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head } }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_footprint_nodes }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_work >= var:eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_budget }
                eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 2 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_naval_layout_collect_one = yes
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes = 0 } eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_finished = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 3 }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_choose = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 4 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_naval_layout_apply_one = yes
        }
    }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes = 0 }
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = {
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_round }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_planning_gain value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain }
                }
                change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_round add = 1 }
                change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_completed add = 1 }
                remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_force_keep
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 3 }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 5 }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_publish = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 6 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_naval_layout_collect_exclusive = yes
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes = 0 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 3 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 7 }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_find_unchecked_candidate = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 8 }
            ordered_owned_location = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old var:eu5ab_naval_layout_compared_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
                order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order
                max = 64
                check_range_bounds = no
                eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_old_seat = yes
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_pending = 0 }
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_checked }
                change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_candidates add = 1 }
                remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_single
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_resume_phase }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 9 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_naval_layout_reduce_full_plan = yes
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes = 0 } eu5ab_naval_layout_select_proposal = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 10 }
while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
eu5ab_naval_layout_prepare_single_winner = yes
        }
    }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes = 0 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 7 }
                eu5ab_naval_layout_start_source_wave = yes
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 11 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }

        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source }
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old
                OR = {
                    root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 2
                    AND = {
                        OR = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 6 root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 7 }
                        NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected }
                    }
                }
            }
            change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value subtract = 80 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode = 7 has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } }
            change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value add = 80 }
        }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value value = { value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value min = 0 max = 100 } }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_seed = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value
     root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 1 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 12 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
if = { limit = { owner ?= root }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_from
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_before

        if = { limit = { root = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready } } }
            eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_location_cache = yes
            eu5ab_naval_planner_prepare_location_value = yes

            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_native_source value = modifier:local_proximity_source }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = -1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_scored_round value = -1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_source value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_source }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_distance value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_band value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order value = -0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_base value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_current value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_best_governor value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_second_governor value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_retained value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_generation value = -1 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_compared_generation value = -1 }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_best_origin
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old_wave_done
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_checked
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_eligible
        if = {
            limit = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_eligible
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seats add = 1 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_eligible
        }
     } else = { root = { eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes } } root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 13 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 13 }

        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
