            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }

        # An admissible upper bound; no live-Proximity shortlist can exclude a site.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_upper_gain value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_planner_total_value }
     root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_min_gain value = 0.00001 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 2 }
    eu5ab_naval_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    eu5ab_naval_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
    eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 14 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    NOT = { owner ?= root }
                    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source != eu5ab_naval_planner_current_proximity_source
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } has_building = building_type:naval_governor }
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } NOT = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes } }
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_snapshot }
        }
     root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 15 } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase = 15 }
            while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor }
        count = 64
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } }

            root = {
                change_variable = {
                    name = eu5ab_naval_layout_after
                    add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_base min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
                }
            }
            set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_live_difference value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_current subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_old_proximity } }
            if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_live_difference < 0 } change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_live_difference multiply = -1 } }
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_planned_vs_live_difference add = { value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_live_difference divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value } } }
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_live_difference
         root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 } }
        }
    }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 5 } }
        }
        else = { eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes }
    }
}

# One pending event per country survives save/load and manual restarts.
eu5ab_naval_layout_schedule_watchdog = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_human = yes
            has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
            NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_scheduled }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_scheduled
        trigger_event_silently = { id = eu5ab_governor.3 days = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_scheduled
    if = {
        limit = { is_human = yes has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks }
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_last_ticks }
                    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_last_ticks
                }
            }
            # One bounded tick per fallback event; GUI and manual use the same work.
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_fallback_batches add = 1 }
            while = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
                count = 1
                eu5ab_naval_layout_tick = yes
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_last_ticks value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_relax_one_planned = {
    var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head = {
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next }
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next } }
        }
        else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
        set_local_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_naval_layout_source_prediction
            value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_road_cost min = 0 }
        }
        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = {
                is_ownable = yes
                owner ?= root

            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
                eu5ab_naval_layout_visit = yes
            }
            if = {
                limit = { eu5ab_is_strait_connection_to = { target = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from } }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_actual_land_edge_prediction }
            }
            else = { set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source_prediction } }
            if = {
                limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed > var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed }
                eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
            }
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_relaxed
        }

        every_neighbor_location = {
            limit = { OR = { topography = lakes topography = high_lakes topography = salt_pans } }
            every_neighbor_location = {
                limit = { is_ownable = yes owner ?= root NOT = { this = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_edge_from }  }
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation } var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation } }
                    eu5ab_naval_layout_visit = yes
                }
                set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed value = eu5ab_naval_planner_lake_transit_prediction }
                if = {
                    limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed > var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed }

                    eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
                }
                remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_lake_relaxed
            }
        }

    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_source_prediction
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_wave_work add = 1 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work add = 1 }
}
# One wave per old source builds its exclusive contribution. Each new
# candidate footprint then evaluates all single replacements without repropagating
# the retained empire. All point and old-seat reductions run in bounded batches.
eu5ab_naval_layout_recover_transport_restart = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_resume_manual }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_resume_manual
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_human = yes
            has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
            OR = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run }
        }
        # Recovery must not turn an automatic job into a manual override.
        eu5ab_naval_planner_run_recalculation_now = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_next_old_source = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_source_origin
    ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old_wave_done } }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order
        max = 1
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old_wave_done
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_old_source
        root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_source_origin value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_old_source } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_source_origin }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_reset_wave = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 5 }
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source_origin = {
            # Preserve other source buildings sharing this old seat.
            set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_source min = 0 max = 100 } }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_seed = yes
            remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_seed_value
        }
    }
    else = {
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = num_locations }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 6 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_collect_exclusive = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_best_origin }
        set_local_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive_gain
            value = {
                value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_current
                subtract = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_base min = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_second_governor }
                min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value
            }
        }
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_best_origin = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive add = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive_gain }
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive_gain
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_collect_single_gain = {
    set_local_variable = {
        name = eu5ab_naval_layout_common_gain
        value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_current min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_common add = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_common_gain } }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_best_origin }
        set_local_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_gain
            value = {
                value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted
                subtract = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_base min = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_second_governor }
                min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value
                subtract = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_common_gain
            }
        }
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_best_origin = {
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_generation != root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery value = 0 }
            }
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery add = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_gain }
        }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_gain
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_common_gain
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_compare_old_seat = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_compared_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_common subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive } }
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery_generation = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
        change_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net add = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_recovery }
    }
    # Save the best origin for every distinct destination, even below the gate.
    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_origin
    root = {
        var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net }
                        local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net > var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net
                    }
                }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_from value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_origin }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_before value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_origin.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net > root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_net
            local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive multiply = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 }
        }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_net value = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_origin value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_target value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_swap_net
    root = {
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_pending subtract = 1 }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_comparisons add = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_find_unchecked_candidate = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate
    if = {
        limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats > 0 }
        ordered_owned_location = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old }
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_checked }
                eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes
            }
            order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order
            max = 1
            save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_unchecked_candidate
            root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_unchecked_candidate } }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_only
        eu5ab_naval_layout_evaluate_contender = yes
    }
    else = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_only
        # Full-plan threshold excludes the contribution of retained old seats.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 6 }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_start_source_wave = yes
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_reduce_full_plan = {
    root = {
        change_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_naval_layout_after
            add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_base min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution
            add = { value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_current subtract = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_retained min = 0 divide = 100 multiply = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
        }
        change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_select_proposal = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_net value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_after subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_before } }
    # Quantize the plan comparison to 0.00001 point for stable ties. This is
    # our deterministic comparison policy, not an assumption about the engine.
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_advantage value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_net subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_net multiply = 100000 round = yes } }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_full_qualifies
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:eu5ab_naval_layout_net > 0
            var:eu5ab_naval_layout_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_full_qualifies
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_target
            OR = {
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_full_qualifies }
                local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_advantage > 0
            }
        }
        # Ties retain the qualifying full plan. Recompute winning coverage so
        # published scores and rows refer to exactly the same source set.
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_proposal_kind value = 2 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_origin.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_exclusive }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_destination_gain value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_net add = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = num_locations }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 10 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
    }
    else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 14 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_snapshot
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_after value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_planned_vs_live_difference value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head } }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_advantage
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_prepare_single_winner = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_virtual value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_planning_gain
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old this != root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_origin }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { this = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_target }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected
    }
    root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes subtract = 1 } }
}

# Called only after every source wave and every planning round completed.
eu5ab_naval_layout_publish = {
    eu5ab_naval_planner_check_transport_rules = yes
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_recover_transport_restart = yes
    }
    else = {
    eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_naval_slots = yes
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                num_locations != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_count
                capital != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_capital
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_capacity != eu5ab_naval_planner_relocation_capacity
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_occupied != eu5ab_naval_planner_relocation_occupied
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_work_generation != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_generation
            }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_snapshot
    }

    # The detailed pass can span pulses. Recheck ownership/native sources and
    # selected seats atomically with publication, without recalculating scores.
    every_in_list = {
        variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_locations
        if = {
            limit = { OR = {
                NOT = { owner ?= root }
                var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_native_source != modifier:local_proximity_source
                AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor } }
                AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } has_building = building_type:naval_governor }
                AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } NOT = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes } }
            } }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_snapshot }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_snapshot }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        # Demolishing one seat cannot free a replacement slot when already over cap.
        limit = { eu5ab_naval_planner_relocation_occupied > eu5ab_naval_planner_relocation_capacity }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_published_results = yes
        eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_local_migration = yes
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready
        eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_liveness = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty }
        # Dirty state belongs to both recommendation consumers. Refresh the
        # synchronous cards before layout_begin consumes it for the next plan.
        eu5ab_naval_planner_recalculate = yes
    }
    else = {

        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_net value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_after subtract = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_before } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies
        if = {
            limit = {
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_net > 0
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_changed_contribution multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 }
            }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies
        }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_publish_rows = yes
        eu5ab_naval_layout_validate_rows = yes
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows }
            # Never combine a complete score/alert with a partial or mismatched list.
            eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_published_results = yes
            eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
        }
        else = {
            eu5ab_naval_layout_confirm = yes
            # UI reads only these published values, never work-in-progress fields.
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_before value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_before }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_road_tier value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_after value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_before }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_net value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_kind value = 0 }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_proposal
            if = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies }
                set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_proposal
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_after value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_after }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_net value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_kind value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_proposal_kind }
            }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_has_results
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_has_results }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_refresh_page = yes
            eu5ab_naval_layout_publish_candidates = yes
            if = {
                limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_has_results }
                set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_show_candidates
                remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_show_new
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh value = 0 }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_ready
            eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_liveness = yes
        }
    }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_confirm = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_seats } var:eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_seats != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats } }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed
    }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_seats value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats }
    every_owned_location = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_target } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected } has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_target }
                    AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_old } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_old }
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_target
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_old
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_target }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_old }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies } }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
    }
    else_if = {
        # An explicit request publishes a qualifying plan immediately. The
        # confirmation/cooldown rules below apply only to unsolicited alerts.
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
        cmf_show_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
    }
    else = {
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_changed NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak } } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak value = 1 }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
            cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
        }
        else = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak add = 1 } }
        if = {
            limit = {
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak >= 2
                NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration }
                OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_migration_cooldown } var:eu5ab_naval_migration_cooldown <= 0 }
            }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_migration
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_migration_cooldown value = 12 }
            cmf_show_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_relocation_alert }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_publish_rows = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_gain
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_matched
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display
        if = {
            limit = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies } has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_selected }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { root = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies } } has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display
        }
        # Reserve retained origins before matching ANY new destination.
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_matched }
    }
    ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_band_order
        # Ordered effects default to one item, not the whole filtered list.
        max = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats
        check_range_bounds = no
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination
        root = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count add = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old } }
                scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination = { eu5ab_naval_layout_store_row = yes }
            }
            else = {
                every_owned_location = {
                    limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_matched } }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_match_score value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_distance subtract = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_distance } }
                    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_match_score > 0 } change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_match_score multiply = -1 } }
                }
                ordered_owned_location = {
                    limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_matched } }
                    order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_match_score
                    max = 1
                    eu5ab_naval_layout_store_row = yes
                }
            }
        }
    }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page value = 0 }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_store_row = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_matched
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_gain value = 0 }
    if = {
        limit = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies } }
        if = {
            limit = { root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_proposal_kind = 2 }
            # Single swaps have a conditional gain, not a greedy-round gain.
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_gain
        }
        else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_gain value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_planning_gain } }
    }
}

# Validate the one-to-one origin/destination mapping before publishing scores
# or showing an alert. Counting the destinations alone misses failed matches.
eu5ab_naval_layout_validate_rows = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_display_count value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_stored_rows value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_validated_moves value = 0 }
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination_seen
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display }
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_display_count add = 1 } }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old
                OR = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to } }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows }
        }
    }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order }
        root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_stored_rows add = 1 } }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_old }
                    NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
                    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order < 1
                    var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order > root.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count
                }
            }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            if = {
                limit = { this != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
                root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_validated_moves add = 1 } }
            }
            var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to = {
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = { NOT = { owner ?= root } NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display } has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination_seen }
                    }
                    root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows }
                }
                set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination_seen
            }
        }
    }
    every_owned_location = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_display NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination_seen } }
            root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows }
        }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_destination_seen
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_display_count != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_stored_rows != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_seats
                AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies var:eu5ab_naval_layout_validated_moves = 0 }
                AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_qualifies } var:eu5ab_naval_layout_validated_moves > 0 }
            }
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_invalid_rows
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_refresh_page = {
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_count value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_result_count divide = 3 ceiling = yes min = 1 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page min = 0 max = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_count subtract = 1 } } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_number value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page add = 1 } }
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_retained
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_order
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page multiply = 3 add = 1 } }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_from value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_to value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_order value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order }
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_list target = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_from = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_to } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_1_retained }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_retained
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_order
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page multiply = 3 add = 2 } }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_from value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_to value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_order value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order }
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_list target = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_from = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_to } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_2_retained }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_list
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_retained
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_from
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_to
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_order
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page multiply = 3 add = 3 } }
    every_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order = local_var:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row }
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_origin
        root = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_from value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_page_origin }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_to value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_order value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_order }
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_list target = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_row_to }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_from = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_to } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_view_3_retained }
        }
    }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_page_row
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_publish_candidates = {
    eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_candidates = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_result_threshold_percent value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent }
    ordered_owned_location = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_from eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes }
        order_by = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net
        max = 3
        check_range_bounds = no
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target
        root = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_count add = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_count = 1 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_from value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_from }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_to value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target }
                add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_list target = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_before value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_before }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_net value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_after value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_before add = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_net } }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_before > 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_net divide = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_before multiply = 100 } }
                }
                else = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_zero_base }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_net > 0 var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_before multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 } }
                    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_1_qualifies
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_count = 2 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_from value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_from }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_to value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target }
                add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_list target = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_before value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_before }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_net value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_after value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_before add = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_net } }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_before > 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_net divide = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_before multiply = 100 } }
                }
                else = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_zero_base }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_net > 0 var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_before multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 } }
                    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_2_qualifies
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_count = 3 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_from value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_from }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_to value = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target }
                add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_list target = scope:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_target }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_before value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_before }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_net value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_layout_preview_net }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_after value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_before add = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_net } }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_before > 0 }
                    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_percent value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_net divide = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_before multiply = 100 } }
                }
                else = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_zero_base }
                if = {
                    limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_net > 0 var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_net >= { value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_before multiply = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_threshold_percent divide = 100 } }
                    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_candidate_3_qualifies
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_initialize_threshold_setting = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { AND = { has_variable_map = cmm trigger_if = { limit = { has_variable_map = cmm } is_key_in_variable_map = { name = cmm target = flag:eu5ab_regional_development__naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent } } } } }
        cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent value = 80 }
    }
}

# CMM supplies +/-1 and Ctrl +/-5 natively. Shift writes an endpoint before
# the callback; convert only this numeric setting's endpoint to a +/-10 step.
# Normal/Ctrl clicks that reach a bound produce the same clamped result.
# Reset writes 80, not an endpoint, so it is never reinterpreted.
eu5ab_naval_planner_adjust_threshold_step = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent }
        if = {
            limit = {
                "variable_map(cmm|flag:eu5ab_regional_development__naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent)" = 200
                var:eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent < 200
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step value = { value = var:eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent add = 10 max = 200 } }
            cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent value = var:eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                "variable_map(cmm|flag:eu5ab_regional_development__naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent)" = 0
                var:eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent > 0
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step value = { value = var:eu5ab_global_naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent subtract = 10 min = 0 } }
            cmm_set_setting_value = { setting = eu5ab_regional_development__naval_governor_migration_threshold_percent value = var:eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step }
        }
    }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_cmm_threshold_step
}

# Manual refresh drains the same planner without GUI/date delays.
# A malformed job must terminate explicitly instead of leaving a pending task.
eu5ab_naval_layout_finish_manual = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
        while = {
            limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
            count = 100000
            eu5ab_naval_layout_tick = yes
            eu5ab_naval_layout_check_liveness = yes
        }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes }
    }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } } remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_liveness = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_liveness_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_stalled_checks
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_0
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_1
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_2
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_3
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_4
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_5
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_6
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_7
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_8
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_9
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_10
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_progress = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_liveness_ready
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_stalled_checks value = 0 }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_phase value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_0 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_mode value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_1 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_round } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_round value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_2 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_round }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_3 value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_4 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_5 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_completed } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_completed value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_6 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_completed }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_candidates } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_candidates value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_7 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_candidates }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_comparisons } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_single_comparisons value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_8 value = var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_comparisons }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_9 value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_work } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_work value = 0 } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_10 value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_work }
}

eu5ab_naval_layout_check_liveness = {
    if = {
        limit = { is_human = yes has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active }
        if = {
            # Old saves have no observation. Give them a full observation window.
            limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_liveness_ready } }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { OR = {
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_phase != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_0
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_mode != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_1
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_round != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_2
                var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_3
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_total_work != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_4
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_pending_nodes != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_5
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_completed != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_6
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_candidates != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_7
                var:eu5ab_naval_layout_single_comparisons != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_8
                var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_9
                var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_work != var:eu5ab_naval_layout_observed_10
            } }
            eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
        }
        else = {
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_stalled_checks add = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_layout_stalled_checks >= 2 }
                eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
            }
        }
    }
}


# A refresh publishes only after validation. Previous cards survive partial work.
eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_begin = {
    eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_transport = yes

    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_capacity value = modifier:num_naval_governors }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_occupied value = 0 }

    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_new
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_layout
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_baseline_failed
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_exhausted
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_failed
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_runs } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_runs value = 0 } }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_runs add = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_total_value value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_work value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_candidates value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_seats value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_snapshot_count value = num_locations }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_snapshot_capital value = capital }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_watchdog_last_ticks value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_fallback_batches value = 0 }
    eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_locations = yes
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
    eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_progress = yes
    eu5ab_naval_layout_schedule_watchdog = yes
}

# Only lightweight links are built synchronously; expensive per-location work
# advances through the saved cursor without repeatedly sorting the whole realm.
eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_locations = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_tail
    clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_locations
    every_owned_location = {
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next
        save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_node
        root = {
            add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_locations target = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_node }
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_tail }
                var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_node } }
            }
            else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_node } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_tail value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_node }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_append_candidate = {
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_next
    save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_new_candidate
    root = {
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_tail }
            var:eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_next value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_new_candidate } }
        }
        else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_new_candidate } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_tail value = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_new_candidate }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_tick = {
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_layout_ticks add = 1 }
    change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_work add = 1 }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_recommendations_enabled <= 0 NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 1 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor any_buildings_in_location = { building_type = building_type:naval_governor building_levels_under_construction > 0 } } }
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_occupied add = 1 } }
        }

        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_score
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_eligible
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_old
        if = { limit = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_eligible
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_candidates add = 1 } }
        }
        if = { limit = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor }
            set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_old
            root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_seats add = 1 } }
        }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

            if = { limit = { eu5ab_fresh_naval_governor_slot_available = yes var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_candidates > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_new }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_seats > 0 var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 OR = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_migration_enabled > 0 has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_layout }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 9 }
            if = { limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_new has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_layout } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 2 } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 2 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }
if = { limit = { owner ?= root }
        eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_location_cache = yes
        eu5ab_naval_planner_prepare_location_value = yes

        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_source value = eu5ab_naval_planner_current_proximity_source }
     } else = { root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid } }
        }

        count = 32

            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_planned_proximity value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = 0 }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = 0 }

            limit = { local_var:eu5ab_naval_gov_baseline_seed > 0 }

            eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
        }

        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 4 } }

        while = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } count = 32

    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 5 }

        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_tail
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 6 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
        }

    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 6 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }
if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_eligible } eu5ab_naval_planner_append_candidate = yes }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 7 }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 7 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_kind value = 2 }
            var:eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
		clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation } }
			set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation value = 0 }
		}
		change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation add = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_value value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage value = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted value = { value = eu5ab_naval_planner_current_proximity_source add = 80 min = 0 max = 100 } }
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_depth value = 0 }
	root = { eu5ab_naval_planner_record_visited = { node = scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate } }
	eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_enqueue = yes
