            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_wave_work value = 0 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 8 }
        }
        else = {
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 9 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_head }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 8 }
        while = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } count = 32
            eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_relax_one = yes
            change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_wave_work add = 1 }
        }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 10 }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head }
        }
        else_if = { limit = { NOT = { eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_wave_budget_available = yes } } eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_failed = yes }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 10 }
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head = { save_temporary_scope_as = eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

			if = {
				limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_visit_generation = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_generation }
				if = {
					limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_predicted > eu5ab_naval_planner_comparison_proximity }
					if = {
						limit = { this ?= scope:eu5ab_naval_gov_evaluated_candidate }
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain add = prev.eu5ab_naval_planner_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					else = {
						root = { change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain add = prev.eu5ab_naval_planner_node_marginal_gain } }
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_value add = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_node_value }
						change_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage add = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_in_queue
			remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_next

        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }
            var:eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head = {

	# Quantize each displayed component first so the one-decimal card values
	# add exactly to the displayed overall score.
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_site_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain
		value = { value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain multiply = 10 round = yes divide = 10 }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_gain
		value = { value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_site_gain add = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
	}
	set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_coverage value = root.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage }
	root = {
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_head
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_queue_tail
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_site_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_surrounding_gain
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_value
		remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_coverage
	}


        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_score value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_gain }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_site_gain value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_site_gain }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_surrounding_gain value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_surrounding_gain }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_coverage value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_result_coverage }

                if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_next } } }
                else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_candidate_head } }
            }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 7 }
        }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase = 9 }
        while = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor }
        count = 32
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor = {
            if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } root = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor value = prev.var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_next } } }
            else = { root = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } }

        if = { limit = { OR = {
            NOT = { owner ?= root }
            AND = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready } var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_native_source != modifier:local_proximity_source }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor } }
            AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_old } has_building = building_type:naval_governor }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_eligible NOT = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes } }
            AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_eligible } eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes }
        } } root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid } }
        }
    }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_cursor } } eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_commit = yes }
    }
    else = { eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_failed = yes }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_failed = {
    set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_exhausted
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_status value = 4 }
    eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_refresh_commit = {

    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_capacity value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_capacity }
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_occupied value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_occupied }
    eu5ab_naval_planner_snapshot_naval_slots = yes
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_capacity != local_var:eu5ab_refresh_capacity } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid }
    if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_occupied != local_var:eu5ab_refresh_occupied } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid }

    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_occupied
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_capacity
    every_in_list = {
        variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_snapshot_locations
        if = { limit = { OR = {
            NOT = { owner ?= root }
            AND = { root = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready } var:eu5ab_naval_gov_snapshot_native_source != modifier:local_proximity_source }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_old NOT = { has_building = building_type:naval_governor } }
            AND = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_old } has_building = building_type:naval_governor }
            AND = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_eligible NOT = { eu5ab_naval_governor_candidate = yes } }
        } } root = { set_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid } }
    }
    if = { limit = { OR = {
        has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_invalid
        has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
        num_locations != var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_snapshot_count
        capital != var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_snapshot_capital
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier != eu5ab_naval_planner_highest_road_tier
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_global_speed != modifier:global_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_land_speed != modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital_speed_propagation
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_road_base != modifier:road_cost_on_distance_from_capital
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_land_base != modifier:land_cost_on_distance_from_capital
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_cached_lake_base != modifier:sea_cost_on_distance_from_capital_when_maritime
    } }
        # Abort clears overrides; restore the manual intent explicitly below.
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_manual value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run } set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_manual value = 1 } }
        eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes
        if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_refresh_manual > 0 } set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_resume_manual }
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
        eu5ab_naval_layout_recover_transport_restart = yes
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_manual
    }
    else = {
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_total value = var:eu5ab_naval_planner_total_value }
        eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_naval_results = yes
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_total_value value = local_var:eu5ab_refresh_total }
        remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_total
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_new } eu5ab_naval_planner_rank_local_results = yes }
    eu5ab_naval_planner_evaluate_local_alerts = yes
    if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_has_new_recommendation } set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_status value = 5 } }
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_baseline_failed has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_queue_exhausted } }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_status value = 4 }
    }
    else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_result_road_tier value = var:eu5ab_naval_gov_transport_road_tier } }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_planner_months_since_refresh value = 0 }
    remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty

        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_new } }
            set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_status value = 1 }
            if = { limit = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_fresh_naval_capacity <= 0 }
                set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_sync_status value = 6 }
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_phase value = 0 }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_run_layout has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_need_layout }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_run_layout
            eu5ab_naval_layout_begin = yes
        }
        else = {
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_run_layout
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_inputs_ready
            eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_liveness = yes
            if = { limit = { OR = { var:eu5ab_naval_gov_refresh_seats = 0 NOT = { var:eu5ab_global_governor_relocation_enabled > 0 } } }
                eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_published_results = yes
                eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_local_migration = yes
            }
        }
    }
}

eu5ab_naval_planner_record_visited = {
    add_to_variable_list = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations target = $node$ }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail }
        var:eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next value = $node$ } }
    }
    else = { set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_head value = $node$ } }
    $node$ = { remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_next }
    set_variable = { name = eu5ab_naval_gov_footprint_tail value = $node$ }
}

# Discard incompatible work once, without changing CMM, mute or cooldown values.
eu5ab_governor_ensure_planner_schema = {
    # An unset variable cannot be compared safely, even inside NOT.
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_planner_schema }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { var:eu5ab_planner_schema = 4 } }
            remove_variable = eu5ab_planner_schema
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = eu5ab_planner_schema } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_scan_active } eu5ab_layout_abort = yes }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_scan_active } eu5ab_naval_layout_abort = yes }
        eu5ab_layout_clear_published_results = yes
        eu5ab_naval_layout_clear_published_results = yes
        eu5ab_governor_clear_country_results = yes
        eu5ab_governor_clear_local_migration = yes
        eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_migration = yes
        eu5ab_governor_clear_naval_results = yes
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_local_governor_available_alert }
        cmf_remove_alert = { alert = eu5ab_naval_governor_available_alert }
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_confirmation_streak
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_confirmation_streak
        remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_failed
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_failed
        remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_resume_manual
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_resume_manual
        remove_variable = eu5ab_governor_manual_run
        remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_manual_run
        clear_variable_list = eu5ab_gov_eval_locations
        clear_variable_list = eu5ab_naval_gov_eval_locations
        every_owned_location = {
            eu5ab_governor_clear_location_cache = yes
            eu5ab_naval_planner_clear_location_cache = yes
            remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_target
            remove_variable = eu5ab_layout_previous_old
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_target
            remove_variable = eu5ab_naval_layout_previous_old
        }
        set_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty
        set_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty
        set_variable = { name = eu5ab_planner_schema value = 4 }
    }
}

# Snapshot dispatch intent before either consumer clears its own dirty flag.
eu5ab_governor_run_scheduled_planners = {
    eu5ab_governor_ensure_planner_schema = yes
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_local value = 0 }
    set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_naval value = 0 }
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_governor_dirty } set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_local value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = eu5ab_naval_planner_dirty } set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_naval value = 1 } }
    }
    else = {
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_local value = 1 }
        set_local_variable = { name = eu5ab_refresh_naval value = 1 }
    }
    if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_refresh_local = 1 } eu5ab_governor_run_recalculation_now = yes }
    if = { limit = { local_var:eu5ab_refresh_naval = 1 } eu5ab_naval_planner_run_recalculation_now = yes }
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_local
    remove_local_variable = eu5ab_refresh_naval
}
